  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一描述不同相对论动力学形式（IFD与LFD）下的对称性结构，揭示共形对称性在动力学插值中的行为，特别是运动学生成元数量最大化对简化哈密顿量构造的意义。

Method: 基于(1+1)维插值共形代数的前期工作，将SO(4,2)代数在(3+1)维中按插值角δ参数化；对15个生成元依其对插值时间坐标的依赖性进行运动学/动力学分类；构造6×6射影时空矩阵表示以实现代数的显式实现。

Result: 1. 插值共形代数SO(4,2)在(3+1)维中成功构建；2. 标度生成元D在δ∈[0,π/4]全程为运动学；3. SCT中一个生成元在(1+1)维光前极限δ=π/4下运动学，但在(3+1)维中变为动力学；4. 给出完整的6×6插值射影时空表示及所有生成元对插值时间的作用。

Conclusion: 共形对称性的插值结构深刻揭示了LFD在相对论动力学中对运动学生成元的最大化能力，为非微扰强耦合量子场论（如QCD）的光前哈密顿量构造提供了更优对称性基础。

Abstract: We extend the interpolation of the Poincaré algebra between instant form dynamics (IFD) and light-front dynamics (LFD) to the conformal algebra in $(3+1)$ dimensions. Building upon our recent formulation of the interpolating conformal algebra in $(1+1)$ dimensions, we propose an interpolation method for the conformal group $SO(4,2)$. A central feature of this work is classifying the $15$ conformal generators into kinematic and dynamic categories as a function of the interpolation angle ($0 \leq δ\leq \fracπ{4}$). We show that among the five additional generators beyond the Poincaré group, the dilatation generator remains strictly kinematic across the entire interpolation region. We also find that in the exact light-front limit ($δ= \fracπ{4}$), one additional generator from the Special Conformal Transformations (SCT) becomes kinematic in $(1+1)$ but dynamical in $(3+1)$. This behavior shows the advantage of LFD in maximizing kinematic generators and minimizing dynamical complexity. To support this algebraic framework, we construct a $6 \times 6$ interpolating projective spacetime matrix representation. We detail the explicit transformations of the interpolating time under all $15$ generators, providing a systematic view of conformal symmetry across the relativistic dynamics.

</details>


### [103] [Emergent Symmetries in Ensemble Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36542)
*Masahito Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在量子引力中全局对称性如何通过理论系综平均而涌现，以二维Narain CFT系综为精确案例，利用Siegel-Weil公式将其解释为三维Abelian Chern-Simons理论中对几何的求和；T对偶性在系综平均后‘折叠’为单个理论的全局对称性（duality origami）；并联系Swampland纲领、无穷远极限下的对称性涌现，以及混合态强-弱自发对称性破缺（SWSSB）与系综平均间的关系，提出新的Swampland猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验量子引力中‘无精确全局对称性’信条的边界，探究系综平均（如全息对偶中的AdS/CFT系综）是否可导致有效全局对称性的涌现，并建立其与Swampland约束、对偶性结构及热场双重态（TFD）虫洞机制的深层联系。

Method: 运用Siegel-Weil公式计算偶二次型Narain CFT的系综平均，将其映射至三维U(1)^c Chern-Simons理论的几何配分函数；分析系综中T-对偶性作为系综内理论间的映射，在平均后退化为单个有效理论的全局对称性（duality origami）；结合热场双重态、ER=EPR框架与混合态SWSSB，将系综平均诠释为对称性涌现的物理机制。

Result: 证明Narain系综平均后涌现离散全局对称群（如O(d,d;ℤ)子群），对应于bulk任何子的电-磁对称性；揭示无穷远极限下该对称性增强现象；确立SWSSB与系综平均的等价性，并指出Einstein-Rosen桥（即Wormhole）是其实现的holographic bulk机制。

Conclusion: 全局对称性虽不在单个量子引力理论中精确存在，但可在适当系综平均下作为普适、可观测的有效对称性涌现；该现象构成Swampland纲领的新维度，提示‘系综等效原理’可能为量子引力基本原理之一，并支持将虫洞几何视为系综相干性的宏观体现。

Abstract: It is widely believed that there are no exact global symmetries in quantum gravity. We review recent works showing that global symmetries can nevertheless emerge after ensemble averages of theories, as encountered in holography. As a precision case study, we discuss the ensemble average of two-dimensional Narain-type conformal field theories associated with an even quadratic form of general signature, which is computed by the Siegel-Weil formula and interpreted as a sum over geometries in a three-dimensional Abelian Chern-Simons theory. Global symmetries of the bulk anyons can emerge after the average, as T-dualities relating different theories in the ensemble are "folded" into symmetries of a single theory (duality origami). We discuss the relation to the Swampland program, including the emergence of global symmetries at infinite distance. Finally, we recast strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) of mixed states as an emergence of symmetries in ensemble averages; in holography, wormholes connecting the two sides of a thermofield-double construction provide its bulk realization. This motivates a new Swampland conjecture.

</details>


### [104] [Classical Shadows of Higher-Form BRST Anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36548)
*Ruizhi Shen,Fu-Wen Shu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种精确意义下BRST反常在经典相空间中存在‘影子’的观点：该影子即协变相空间上哈密顿对称作用的非等变类；为此引入Weil协变相空间双复形，其基本子复形控制等变哈密顿提升；进而将该经典类与局域BRST下降进行比较；对哈密顿容许混合反常（mixed transgression），ghost数为2的下降元a²_{d−1}定义了去鬼化映射至电荷代数，该代数由Cartan代表元的非等变性生成；所诱导的上同调类在Bardeen型下降代表元变换下不变；因此经典反常与BRST反常是同一Weil反常类在不同理论中的实现。文末以五维流入反常为例验证该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立BRST量子反常与经典相空间几何结构之间的精确对应，揭示其共有的Weil上同调起源，弥合理论物理中量子与经典反常描述的割裂。

Method: 运用微分几何与代数拓扑工具，构建Weil协变相空间双复形，分析其基本子复形对等变哈密顿提升的控制作用；结合BRST上同调与局部下降方程，定义去鬼化映射并证明其诱导的上同调类在Bardeen变换下的不变性；通过五维流入反常的具体计算验证理论框架。

Result: 证明经典相空间中哈密顿对称作用的非等变类与BRST反常共享同一Weil反常类（Weil transgression class）；构造了从ghost数2下降元a²_{d−1}到电荷代数的规范不变去鬼化映射；该映射所诱导的上同调类独立于Bardeen型下降代表元的选择；五维流入反常实例确认了两种反常作为同一Weil类之不同实现的一致性。

Conclusion: BRST反常与经典相空间非等变性并非孤立现象，而是同一Weil上同调类在量子（BRST）与经典（Hamiltonian covariant phase space）两个范畴中的不同实现；该统一视角为理解反常匹配、反常流入及全息对偶中的对称性实现提供了严格的几何基础。

Abstract: We formulate a precise sense in which a BRST anomaly may possess a classical phase space shadow. It is the non-equivariance class of a Hamiltonian symmetry action on covariant phase space. To organize this statement, we introduce the Weil covariant phase space bicomplex, whose basic subcomplex controls equivariant Hamiltonian lifts. We then compare this purely classical class with local BRST descent. For a Hamiltonian admissible mixed transgression, the ghost-number-two descendant $a^{2}_{d-1}$ defines a deghostification map to the charge algebra, which is generated by the failure of the Cartan representative to be equivariant. The induced cohomology class is independent of Bardeen type changes of descent representative. Thus the classical anomaly is a shadow of the BRST anomaly in the precise sense that both are realizations of the same Weil transgression class, while remaining objects of different theories. As an explicit example, we analyze a five dimensional inflow transgression and show that the classical charge cocycle and the BRST descendant arise as two realizations of the same Weil transgression class.

</details>


### [105] [Color Relations and Off-Shell Double-Copy for Towers of Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36604)
*James Mangan,Able Martinez,Joseph Thomas*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将双拷贝（double copy）推广至无限族完全反对称的多指标'结构常数'，提出其Jacobi恒等式，并构建了与之色对偶的导数耦合标量理论塔（含Zakharov-Mikhailov理论为基例）及修正的非阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论塔；所有理论均具色-动量对偶守恒流、软定理、壳上递推关系与经典共形不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展双拷贝框架，使其容纳超越传统三指标结构常数f^{abc}的高阶完全反对称n-指标‘结构常数’，以探索更广义的色-动量对偶代数结构及其对应的场论实现。

Method: 基于n-指标完全反对称张量定义广义Jacobi恒等式；构造一族导数耦合标量理论（每维时空对应一理论），验证其离壳色对偶性；构造相应修正的非阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论，分析其拓扑性、共形性与动力学特征（如动能混合）；识别确保离壳色对偶性的特殊规范条件。

Result: 确立n-指标结构常数的Jacobi型代数闭包；给出首个离壳色-动量对偶的无限维标量理论塔（含2D Zakharov-Mikhailov为n=3情形）；构建对应Chern-Simons理论塔，证明其经典共形不变性与平凡拓扑性，但呈现动能混合等非标准性质；指出一类潜在非物理规范条件可保障离壳对偶性。

Conclusion: 双拷贝结构可系统延拓至任意阶完全反对称结构常数，形成具有统一对称性（共形、软定理、递推）的理论塔；该结构暗示色-动量对偶根植于更普适的高阶李型代数，为探索引力/规范理论新构造提供代数与场论双重框架。

Abstract: Only a handful of elementary color building blocks are known to participate in the double copy, most notably the structure constants of gauge theory. This work extends the double copy to include the infinite family of totally antisymmetric ``structure constants'' with arbitrarily many indices. We propose and motivate the Jacobi identities relating these $n$-index structure constants. For each $n$, we provide a derivatively-coupled scalar theory that is color-dual off-shell. The lowest level in this tower is two-dimensional Zakharov-Mikhailov theory, with one theory for each higher spacetime dimension. Each member of the tower exhibits a conserved current associated with color-kinematics duality, a soft theorem, an on-shell recursion relation, and classical conformal invariance. We also provide a tower of modified non-abelian Chern-Simons theories whose fields couple non-linearly through the $n$-index structure constants. The modified Chern-Simons theories are trivially topological and classically conformal but generally exhibit unusual features like kinetic mixing. A potentially unphysical gauge condition is identified that would ensure that the tower is color-dual off-shell.

</details>


### [106] [Notes on algebraic perspective on neural network conformal theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37074)
*Sam Leutheusser,Joydeep Naskar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络共形理论中关联函数的结构及算符间关系，并提出构建该理论代数的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解神经网络共形理论中关联函数的内在结构、算符谱的简并性及其代数性质。

Method: 基于无限宽神经网络系综计算的精确两点、三点和四点关联函数，分析其交叉对称性、共形块分解及算符谱结构，并推导算符代数构造方案。

Result: 确认四点函数满足交叉对称性与无矩约束下的共形块分解一致性；发现具有相同标度维数的无穷塔简并算符；提出构造该理论算符代数的具体方案。

Conclusion: 神经网络共形理论不仅具备标准共形场论的关键特征（如交叉对称性与共形块分解），还展现出非平凡的简并谱结构与可构造的算符代数，表明其为一类新型、严格可解的共形理论。

Abstract: Neural network conformal theories were first introduced in \cite{Halverson:2024axc} and exact two-, three- and four-point correlators were computed from an ensemble of infinite-width neural networks. In particular, the four-point correlator of any scalar operator satisfied crossing-symmetry and conformal block decomposition across different channels were in agreement without any constraint on moments. An infinite tower of degenerate operators with the same scaling dimension were found. In this note, we take a closer look at the structure of correlators and relation between operators. We also layout a recipe to construct the algebra of the theory.

</details>


### [107] [Dirichlet gravitons in AdS$_4$ and $\mathcal{L}_Λw_{1+\infty}$ wedge algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36764)
*Hare Krishna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了在AdS₄中、满足Dirichlet边界条件的软引力子代数的AdS变形，通过自旋螺旋度表象、Mellin变换与AdS楔形构造，唯一确定软模对易关系为ℒ_Λw_{1+∞}楔形，其中Λ = −ℓ⁻²，实现了不依赖共线分裂函数或自对偶截断的宇宙学常数变形的体部推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立AdS背景下软引力子代数的严格变形，以兼容AdS几何与Dirichlet边界条件，并规避现有方法（如共线分裂函数或自对偶截断）的近似性与局限性。

Method: 基于AdS₄自旋螺旋度表象，对线性化引力子解作Mellin变换，构造AdS楔形（含Laurent完备化）；结合Dirichlet边界条件（配对相反螺旋度）、AdS协变性（固定相对归一化）、线性闭合假设及全局模归一化以复现几何AdS等距，再由Jacobi恒等式唯一确定软模Poisson括号。

Result: 软模代数结构被唯一确定为ℒ_Λw_{1+∞}的楔形代数，其中宇宙学常数Λ = −ℓ⁻²，该结果精确编码AdS边界条件与曲率效应。

Conclusion: 首次从体部（bulk）第一性原理出发，完整导出AdS₄中Dirichlet边界下的软引力子代数变形，确认其代数本质为deformed w_{1+∞}，为AdS/CFT中软定理与渐近对称性的统一提供了代数基础。

Abstract: We derive the AdS deformation of the flat-space soft-graviton algebra with Dirichlet boundary conditions. Starting from the AdS$_4$ spinor-helicity representation, we Mellin-transform linearized graviton solutions and construct the AdS wedge, including its Laurent completion. The Dirichlet boundary condition pairs opposite helicities, while AdS covariance fixes their relative normalization. Assuming linear closure and normalizing the global modes to reproduce the geometric AdS isometries, covariance and the Jacobi identities uniquely determine the soft-mode bracket. The result is the wedge of $\mathcal L_Λw_{1+\infty}$, where $Λ=-\ell^{-2}$. This provides a bulk derivation of the cosmological-constant deformation that incorporates the AdS boundary condition, without relying on collinear splitting functions or a self-dual truncation.

</details>


### [108] [Diagrammatic construction of GKZ systems for polygonal functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38030)
*K. B. Alkalaev,Semyon Mandrygin,Y. M. Zalishchansky*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究多边形超几何函数，提出其与任意维数下多点参数单圈共形积分的关联，并通过图解算法构建对应的GKZ系统，导出有限阶环面微分方程子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统评估任意维数下多点参数单圈共形积分，需发展具明确几何/组合结构的超几何函数框架；多边形超几何函数为此类积分提供候选解析表示。

Method: 基于多边形幂级数构造对应的Gelfand–Kapranov–Zelevinsky（GKZ）超几何系统；引入‘transianic矩阵’编码图解数据，由此导出环面矩阵与关系格基，从而截取含n(n−3)/2个二阶与三阶方程的有限环面子系统。

Result: 确立了多边形超几何函数与GKZ系统的严格对应；证明其环面微分方程可约化为有限低阶子系统（二阶与三阶为主）；以第四Appell函数和Srivastava–Daoust函数为特例验证该框架。

Conclusion: 多边形超几何函数构成一类具清晰图解结构的GKZ系统，其环面方程具有天然的阶数截断性质，为高维共形积分的解析求解提供了可控的微分方程方法。

Abstract: We investigate the recently introduced polygonal hypergeometric functions [arXiv:2502.12127, arXiv:2507.01904], which are conjectured to evaluate multipoint parametric one-loop conformal integrals in arbitrary dimensions. These functions can be systematically constructed using a diagrammatic algorithm that operates in terms of simple planar shapes. Given an $n$-point polygonal power series, we formulate the corresponding GKZ hypergeometric system, which admits an explicit diagrammatic interpretation. In particular, the transianic matrix, which encodes all the relevant diagrammatic data, determines the corresponding toric matrix along with a basis of the lattice of relations, thereby singling out a finite subsystem of $n(n-3)/2$ toric equations. This finite subsystem consists of second- and third-order equations, whereas the full toric family also includes higher-order ones. We illustrate the general procedure with several prominent examples of polygonal functions, including the fourth Appell and the Srivastava--Daoust functions.

</details>


### [109] [The Origin and Fate of Rapid Negative Modes in Coleman--De Luccia Tunneling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37092)
*Yutaka Ookouchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出Coleman-De Luccia隧穿中出现的无限负模塔并非对应无穷多独立不稳定性，而是源于单一引力共形扇区；通过统一规范与紫外正则化，该高频频谱在假真空与bounce解间一致，从而紫外负模相消，仅剩一个bounce特有负模，为CDL前因子的系统计算扫清概念障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Coleman-De Luccia隧穿半经典路径积分中由无限负模塔引发的概念混淆与技术困难，特别是其对前因子计算的阻碍。

Method: 在统一规范（如谐波/同步规范）和共同紫外正则化方案下，对比分析假真空与bubble bounce背景下的标量-张量涨落谱，识别并分离出源自引力共形扇区的高频负模；利用几何结构（如欧氏紧致几何的转折点、泡壁附近大标量梯度）揭示该扇区的普适性。

Result: 证明无限负模塔本质上是同一共形扇区的多重激发，其高频部分在假真空与bounce之间渐近等价，因此在相对负模计数中完全抵消，仅保留一个物理的bounce特有负模。

Conclusion: Coleman-De Luccia隧穿的负模问题具有深层几何与规范结构根源，通过恰当处理共形扇区可实现紫外安全的前因子计算，为真空衰变率的高精度预测奠定基础。

Abstract: Coleman--De Luccia tunneling can exhibit an infinite tower of rapidly oscillating negative modes, complicating the semiclassical evaluation of the path integral. We show that this tower does not represent infinitely many bounce-specific instabilities, but instead is tied to a single underlying gravitational conformal sector. This sector is exposed in two different ways: by a large scalar gradient near the bubble wall and by the turning point of a compact Euclidean geometry. The corresponding conformal sector is already present in fluctuations about the false vacuum. Treating the bounce and the false vacuum in the same well-defined gauge and with a common regulator, we find that their high-frequency negative spectra approach one another. The ultraviolet tower therefore cancels from the relative negative-mode count, leaving a single bounce-specific negative mode. This clears a major conceptual obstacle toward a systematic calculation of the Coleman--De Luccia prefactor.

</details>


### [110] [Constructing and Probing Three-Boundary Booklet Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37099)
*Yan Liu,Shaodong Zhou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出三边界部分纠缠热态（PETS₃），通过引入三价结点张量推广双边界壳态框架；利用Israel拼接条件约束对应的书页状三边界体几何，并在对称全黑洞扇区验证态计数自洽性；重算符探针给出探测-普适配分函数比 Z_D/Z_U ≈ 3，为两边界情形的两倍，表明单边界局域精细调谐探针可区分PETS₃与PETS₂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展双边界部分纠缠热态（PETS）至三边界情形，以构建更丰富的黑洞性质微观态族，并探索多边界纠缠结构对半经典引力几何及信息探测能力的影响。

Method: 将双指标壳插入数据推广为三价结点张量，构造三边界部分纠缠热态（PETS₃）；应用Israel拼接条件约束其对应bulk书页状三边界几何；在对称全黑洞扇区进行态计数验证；利用重算符作为局域探针计算探测配分函数Z_D与普适配分函数Z_U之比。

Result: 确立了PETS₃的半经典几何实现（三边界booklet geometry），识别出对称与混合取向分支；态计数在对称全黑洞扇区自洽；获得Z_D/Z_U ≃ 3，显著大于PETS₂的3/2；证明单边界局域探针原则上可分辨PETS₃与PETS₂。

Conclusion: PETS₃构成一类新的、具有明确半经典几何实现的多边界黑洞性质微观态；其增强的探测-普适比揭示了多边界纠缠结构对量子引力中信息定位能力的非平庸提升。

Abstract: Partially entangled thermal states (PETS) provide semiclassical families of black-hole microstates prepared through heavy shell insertions. We extend this shell-state framework and propose three-boundary partially entangled thermal states (PETS$_3$) by replacing the two-index insertion data with a trivalent junction tensor. The corresponding bulk three-boundary booklet geometry is constrained semiclassically by the Israel junction condition, allowing us to identify candidate symmetric and mixed-orientation branches. We verify the self-consistency of this construction by counting states in the symmetric all-black-hole sector. Using a heavy-operator probe, we further estimate the detection-to-universal ratio to be $Z_D/Z_U \simeq 3$, twice the two-boundary value of $3/2$. These results suggest that a fine-tuned probe localized on one boundary can test the proposed three-boundary shell state and potentially distinguish it from its two-boundary counterpart.

</details>


### [111] [Active normal-fluid feedback in quantum turbulence from holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37269)
*Yu-Ping An,Li Li,Makoto Tusbota,Sebastian Waeber,Hua-Bi Zeng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过全自洽的引力对偶模型，首次从第一性原理出发模拟了超流体中双流体耦合的量子湍流，揭示了正常流体速度统计的非高斯性与各向异性源于量子涡旋的动力学反作用，并发现升温虽增加涡旋密度却抑制正常流体速度涨落，其根源在于热激发导致超流凝聚体耗尽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统双流体模型中离散涡线描述与连续介质描述之间的根本不兼容性，摆脱对唯象参数的依赖，建立量子湍流的第一性原理理论框架。

Method: 采用全背反应（fully backreacted）的全息引力模型，内禀地实现超流与正常流体间的动量互换及有限温度耗散，无需任何经验输入。

Result: 发现持续驱动与自由衰减湍流下正常流体速度分布呈现显著非高斯尾部和空间各向异性；升温导致正常流体速度涨落被抑制，该效应可明确归因于热致超流凝聚体耗尽。

Conclusion: 全息对偶为量子湍流提供了严格的、第一性原理的理论框架，揭示了远离平衡态时正常流体的主动动力学角色，并为冷原子与超流实验提供了可检验的预测。

Abstract: The two-fluid model provides a powerful phenomenological framework for superfluids, where components interact through vortex-induced mutual friction. Quantum turbulence in such coupled two-fluid systems has become a central theme in low-temperature physics. However, the fundamental incompatibility between discrete vortex-line and continuum descriptions has historically forced reliance on phenomenological parameters. Here, we present an ab initio holographic simulation of this active two-fluid dynamics. Using a fully backreacted gravitational model, we intrinsically incorporate reciprocal momentum exchange and finite-temperature dissipation without empirical inputs. Contrasting continuously driven and freely decaying turbulence, we uncover pronounced non-Gaussian tails and spatial anisotropy in the normal-fluid velocity statistics, arising directly from the dynamical quantized vortex backreaction. Raising the temperature suppresses normal-fluid velocity fluctuations despite increasing the vortex density. This suppression is unambiguously traced to the thermal depletion of the superfluid condensate. Our results establish holographic duality as a rigorous first-principles framework for quantum turbulence, revealing the active role of the normal fluid far from equilibrium and yielding predictions for ongoing cold-atom and superfluid experiments.

</details>


### [112] [Relic of the Kink Shape Mode in a Topological Thick Brane - some implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37346)
*Gahan Chattopadhyay,Soumitra Sengupta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维广义相对论中由标量场驱动的拓扑厚3-膜（kink soliton），指出引力耦合下需引入M_*压制的φ⁶修正以维持稳定翘曲几何；其标量扰动谱无正规化束缚态，但存在一个准束缚（共振）模式，并通过多种方法证明该共振与平直空间φ⁴ kink的束缚形变模直接关联；进一步表明该共振可在有效场论自洽参数窗内实现从体几何涨落到局域物质的能量转移，为早期宇宙高能再加热提供新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力背景下拓扑缺陷（如kink）如何支持稳定的厚膜世界模型，并探究其扰动谱结构与宇宙学应用（如再加热）之间的联系。

Method: 结合时间独立相对概率分析、直接时序演化模拟和四转折点WKB近似计算，分析五维翘曲几何中含φ⁶修正的标量kink解的线性扰动谱，并评估其在EFT框架下的能量转移效率。

Result: 发现引力kink无正规化标量束缚态，但存在唯一准束缚共振态；该共振与平直空间φ⁴ kink的束缚形变模一一对应；在EFT一致参数范围内，该共振可高效介导体几何涨落向局域物质的能量转移。

Conclusion: 五维引力kink厚膜虽缺乏传统束缚态，但其共振结构承载了平直极限下的物理信息，并具备作为高能再加热通道的可行性，凸显了共振在膜世界动力学与早期宇宙学中的关键作用。

Abstract: The kink soliton provides a simple construction of a topological thick 3-brane sourced by a minimally coupled canonical scalar field in five-dimensional general relativity. Although the flat-space kink is a solution of $\varphi^4$ theory, the gravitating configuration requires $M_*$-suppressed $\varphi^6$ corrections to support a stable warped geometry. Unlike its flat-space counterpart, the scalar perturbation spectrum contains no normalizable bound state. We nevertheless identify a single quasi-bound, or resonant, mode. Using a time-independent relative-probability analysis, direct time evolution, and a four-turning-point WKB calculation, we establish a direct connection between this resonance and the massive bound shape mode of the flat-space $\varphi^4$ kink. We also show that, within an EFT-consistent parameter window, the resonance can transfer energy from bulk geometric fluctuations to localized matter, thereby allowing the possibility of high-scale reheating in the early universe.

</details>


### [113] [Can Multipartite Entanglement Probe Wormhole Moduli Invisible to Bipartite Entanglement?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37394)
*Norihiro Iizuka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了多部分纠缠（特别是q=4全息多熵）能否探测到双部分纠缠（如RT熵）无法感知的体空间模量（由Fenchel-Nielsen扭转变量τ表征），在一类四边界AdS₃虫洞中发现：在对称构型(ℓ=m)且|τ|较小时，q=4多熵与τ无关，与双部分RT熵一致，并未提供额外几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验多部分纠缠是否能探测双部分纠缠（如RT熵）所盲区的体空间几何自由度，即是否存在τ-独立的双部分熵但τ-敏感的多熵。

Method: 在四边界AdS₃虫洞族中，利用Fenchel-Nielsen twist τ参数化几何形变；系统比较所有可能的Ryu-Takayanagi极小面配置与q=4全息多熵网络（即q=4的entanglement wedge cross-section类推广），分析其对τ的依赖性，特别聚焦对称线ℓ=m下全局极小网络的竞争与相变行为。

Result: 在对称线ℓ=m且|τ|足够小时，q=4全息多熵与τ完全无关，与双部分RT熵同步保持不变；该结果源于多个候选网络间能量竞争导致的多次相变，且持续至RT熵本身开始响应τ的临界点。

Conclusion: 在所研究的AdS₃四边界虫洞族及对称参数范围内，q=4多部分纠缠并未超越双部分纠缠的几何探测能力——二者对Fenchel-Nielsen模量τ具有相同的鲁棒性，暗示该模量不被任何低阶全息纠缠熵所分辨。

Abstract: We ask whether multipartite entanglement can probe bulk moduli invisible to bipartite entanglement. We study this question in an explicit family of four-boundary AdS$_3$ wormholes related by a Fenchel-Nielsen twist $τ$, asking whether the complete set of bipartite RT entropies can remain exactly $τ$-independent while the holographic $\mathtt q=4$ multi-entropy is sensitive to the twist. Within the class of configurations analyzed here, for sufficiently small $|τ|$, we find no such regime along the symmetric line $\ell=m$: the globally minimal $\mathtt q=4$ network is itself exactly $τ$-independent throughout the parameter range analyzed. This conclusion is not a priori obvious: it emerges from a competition among several admissible configurations whose relative ordering changes multiple times as the geometry is varied, and persists up to the point where the bipartite RT entropies themselves become twist-sensitive.

</details>


### [114] [Multipartite Entanglement Can Probe Wormhole Moduli Invisible to Bipartite Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37395)
*Takanori Anegawa,Norihiro Iizuka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发现，在AdS₃四边界虫洞的有限Fenchel-Nielsen扭转下，尽管所有二分Ryu-Takayanagi熵保持不变，q=4多体熵却能探测到该扭转，揭示了其对体几何信息的独有敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶多体纠缠熵（特别是q=4多熵）是否能编码超出二分纠缠熵的 bulk 几何信息，尤其是在虫洞模空间中扭转参数变化时。

Method: 分析四边界AdS₃ wormhole在Fenchel-Nielsen twist下的RT面构型演化，比较完整二分RT熵向量与q=4 multi-entropy对扭转变量的响应，识别全局极小网络的相变区域。

Result: 发现在模空间的一个开区域内，所有二分RT熵严格不变，而q=4 multi-entropy显式依赖于扭转参数；该效应源于扭转拉长交叉测地线，使一个扭转敏感的q=4网络成为全局极小。

Conclusion: q=4 genuine multi-entropy 可探测被所有二分纠缠熵所‘盲区’的 bulk 几何自由度，为高阶纠缠作为量子引力探针提供了明确例证。

Abstract: In a companion paper, we found that near the untwisted point of a four-boundary AdS$_3$ wormhole, whenever the complete bipartite Ryu-Takayanagi entropy vector is insensitive to a Fenchel-Nielsen twist, the holographic $\mathtt q=4$ multi-entropy is insensitive as well. Here we show that the situation changes at finite twist. The twist lengthens the crossing geodesics and thereby enlarges the region in which all bipartite RT entropies remain invariant. In part of this enlarged region, a twist-sensitive $\mathtt q=4$ network becomes globally minimal. We thus find an open region of wormhole moduli space in which the complete bipartite RT entropy vector is exactly unchanged while the $\mathtt q=4$ multi-entropy detects the twist. Our result provides an explicit example in which the $\mathtt q=4$ genuine multi-entropy probes bulk geometric information invisible to all bipartite entanglement entropies.

</details>


### [115] [Quantum states in an AdS-invariant theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37401)
*Felix M. Lev,Valery E. Lyubovitskij*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出，反德西特（AdS）量子理论中基本粒子的描述与标准方法存在根本差异，关键在于AdS代数的Cartan子代数秩为2，因此必须仅考虑依赖于其不可约表示（IRs）中算符本征值的物理量；而现有AdS/QCD等应用常忽略该代数IRs的非平凡结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示AdS对称性在量子理论中被普遍误用的问题，强调AdS代数不可约表示的特殊结构（如Cartan子代数秩为2）对粒子描述的根本约束，纠正当前AdS/QCD等框架中忽略该数学特性的倾向。

Method: 基于李代数表示论，分析AdS代数so(2,d)的Cartan子代数结构及其不可约表示的本征值依赖性，强调物理可观测量必须显式依赖于表示中Casimir算符和Cartan生成元的本征值。

Result: 确立AdS量子理论中粒子态必须由AdS代数的完整不可约表示刻画，且可观测量仅能依赖于对应IR中Cartan生成元和Casimir算符的本征值；标准平直时空粒子分类（如庞加莱群表示）不能直接推广至AdS背景。

Conclusion: AdS背景下的基本粒子描述必须严格遵循so(2,d)代数的表示论结构，尤其需以Cartan子代数秩2为出发点重构量子态与可观测量的定义；现有AdS/QCD等唯象模型若忽略此结构性约束，则缺乏严格的群表示论基础。

Abstract: We note that the description of elementary particles in anti-de Sitter (AdS) quantum theory differs radically from standard approaches. Since the Cartan subalgebra of the AdS algebra has rank 2, it is necessary to consider only those elements that explicitly depend on the eigenvalues of operators in the irreducible representations (IRs) of the algebra. In the literature, AdS symmetry is widely invoked in various quantum applications, such as AdS/QCD. However, such frameworks typically do not take into account the unconventional properties of the AdS algebra IRs investigated in the present paper.

</details>


### [116] [The Stieltjes Bootstrap for Feynman Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37536)
*Hadrien Brochet,Maximilian Haensch,Johannes Henn,Benjamin Hollering,Prashanth Raman,Qinglin Yang,Simone Zoia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了标量费曼积分在每个运动学变量和粒子质量下均为Stieltjes函数，并利用第二Symanzik多项式的两森林分解给出严格证明；进一步将该性质用于构造满足Stieltjes性质的主积分基，结合Padé逼近、Hankel行列式约束与微分方程，发展出基于半定规划的数值计算方法，并应用于6维单圈盒子积分及三圈双质量电弱修正关键积分的高精度计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立费曼积分解析性质（特别是Stieltjes性）与数值计算之间的桥梁，以提升高阶微扰计算（如三圈电弱修正）的可靠性与效率。

Method: 基于第二Symanzik多项式的spanning two-forest分解，导出非负系数表达式；结合Stieltjes函数判据、IBP约化选取主积分基；融合Hankel行列式约束、微分方程与半定优化进行数值求解。

Result: 证明任意圈数、外腿数、内质量的标量费曼积分为Stieltjes函数（在指数条件满足时）；导出新的Hankel型Taylor系数约束；实现6D盒子积分及三圈双质量Z/Higgs自能积分的高精度数值计算。

Conclusion: Stieltjes结构为费曼积分提供了深层解析约束，可系统提升高阶微扰计算的数值稳定性与收敛性，尤其适用于Euclidean区域的关键物理过程。

Abstract: We prove that scalar Feynman integrals are Stieltjes functions in each kinematic variable $-p_i\cdot p_j$, as well as in the particle masses, for arbitrary loop order, arbitrary numbers of external legs, and arbitrary internal masses, whenever a certain exponent condition is satisfied. The proof rests on a canonical decomposition of the second Symanzik polynomial with manifestly non-negative coefficients indexed by spanning two-forests. We present a dedicated analysis of the case in which the kinematic variables satisfy relations, as frequently happens in physical applications. We argue that integration-by-parts relations allow the selection of master-integral bases in which every element is a Stieltjes function. The Stieltjes property guarantees the convergence of Padé approximants and provides determinantal (Hankel) constraints on the Taylor coefficients of master integrals that go beyond previously known complete-monotonicity bounds. We develop a numerical strategy that combines these Stieltjes constraints with differential equations to determine the master integrals in the Euclidean region via semidefinite optimization. We demonstrate this approach on the pedagogical example of the six-dimensional massive box integral, and then apply it to the calculation of previously unknown three-loop two-mass integrals needed for three-loop electroweak corrections to the $Z$- and Higgs-boson self-energies.

</details>


### [117] [Reconciling Inflation with the Festina Lente Bound through Affleck-Dine Baryogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37674)
*Kazuto Nakamura,Masaki Yamada*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Festina-Lente（FL）swampland猜想对暴胀模型的约束，指出通过在暴胀期间完全破缺所有规范对称性（使规范玻色子质量高于哈勃尺度），可自然规避该约束；并系统分类MSSM中能完全破缺连续规范群的单/双平坦方向，发现所有满足完全破缺的单向单项式均携带非零B−L荷，从而成为Affleck-Dine重子生成的理想候选；进一步证明此类大场强凝聚态在满足观测重子不对称性和等曲率扰动限制的同时，自动满足FL界，建立了FL界与Affleck-Dine机制间的自然联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Festina-Lente bound作为源于dS空间中带电黑洞行为的swampland猜想，虽在当今宇宙较弱，却对暴胀模型构成严峻限制；需厘清其前提并寻找规避机制。

Method: 分析FL bound的底层假设；构造暴胀期间所有规范对称性被完全破缺（规范玻色子质量 m_g > H_inf）的场景；系统枚举MSSM中满足F-和D-flatness且能完全破缺SU(3)_c × SU(2)_L × U(1)_Y的单/双平坦方向；结合提升算符研究其在Affleck-Dine机制中产额重子不对称性的能力；结合等曲率扰动与重子数观测约束检验FL bound满足性。

Result: 1) 规范对称性完全破缺且m_g > H_inf是规避FL bound的有效机制；2) MSSM中所有能单向完全破缺规范群的平坦方向均为含非零B−L的单项式；3) 此类方向支持Affleck-Dine重子生成；4) 满足观测重子不对称性与等曲率限制的大场强condensate自动满足FL bound。

Conclusion: FL bound与Affleck-Dine重子生成在规范对称性破缺框架下存在内在一致性：暴胀期间规范群的完全破缺既规避FL约束，又为B−L驱动的Affleck-Dine机制提供必要条件，二者统一于同一物理图景。

Abstract: The Festina-Lente bound is a swampland conjecture motivated by the behavior of charged BHs in dS spacetime, which imposes a constraint on the masses of charged particles. Although relatively weak in the present-day Universe, it can severely constrain inflation. We clarify its underlying assumptions and show that breaking all gauge symmetries during inflation, with gauge boson masses above the Hubble scale, provides a natural way to evade these constraints. We catalogue single flat directions in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model that completely break its continuous gauge group, together with pairs that achieve complete breaking only when both directions are excited and simultaneously satisfy F- and D-flatness. With suitable lifting operators, such condensates can also generate the baryon asymmetry through the Affleck-Dine mechanism. Within this classification, all single-direction monomials that admit complete breaking carry nonzero $B-L$, providing candidates for Affleck-Dine baryogenesis. We further show that, in this setting, a large condensate compatible with the observed baryon asymmetry and isocurvature constraints readily satisfies the Festina-Lente bound, establishing a natural connection between the FL bound and Affleck-Dine baryogenesis.

</details>


### [118] [On perturbative aspects of a nonminimal Lorentz-violating QED with CPT-odd dimension-6 terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37768)
*T. Mariz,M. Melo,J. R. Nascimento,A. Yu. Petrov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了所有可能的非最小维度6加性CPT-奇洛伦兹破缺（LV）项所诱导的一阶单圈量子修正，重点分析其对Carroll-Field-Jackiw（CFJ）型项的辐射诱导效应；发现某些维度6 LV算符会导致CFJ项发散，但该发散可通过LV参数间的特定关系被抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高维洛伦兹破缺项在量子水平上是否能自洽地诱导出低维（如维度3）CPT-奇有效项（CFJ项），并检验其紫外行为与参数约束。

Method: 采用一阶微扰论框架，在动量截断或维度正则化下计算所有维度6 CPT-奇非最小耦合LV算符贡献的真空极化张量，并提取其轴向矢量部分以识别CFJ型有效项。

Result: 维度6 LV算符可辐射诱导出CFJ项，且该诱导项在一般情况下紫外发散；但存在特定参数关系（如LV张量分量满足的代数约束）可使发散项精确相消，实现有限CFJ项。

Conclusion: 维度6 CPT-奇洛伦兹破缺结构在单圈水平上可自洽诱导CFJ项，其紫外发散性并非本质障碍，而是受LV参数空间中可解约束所调控，暗示此类高维LV模型具有潜在的量子一致性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the first-order one-loop quantum corrections generated by all possible nonminimal dimension-6 additive CPT-odd Lorentz-violating (LV) terms to analyze the radiative induction of the Carroll-Field-Jackiw (CFJ)-like terms. We demonstrate that for dimension-6 operators, the resulting CFJ term can diverge for some choices of LV operators; however, in all these cases, this divergence is eliminated for certain relations between LV parameters.

</details>


### [119] [Monopoles, duality, and large charge in Chern-Simons QED$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37795)
*Shai M. Chester,Éric Dupuis*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文计算了大N和大k极限下QED₃中电荷q磁单极子算符的标度维数（至次领头阶），并利用该结果检验了QED₃与标量QED₃之间的强耦合对偶性，特别是N=1、k=-3/2的QED₃与N=1、k=2的sQED₃之间的SO(3)增强对偶；同时发现高q行为符合普适有效场论预言，且揭示了k≠0时手征性破缺下q⁰项的非平凡普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验QED₃与sQED₃之间由monopole duality预言的强耦合对偶关系，特别是当U(1) monopole symmetry增强为SO(3)时的谱匹配；并探究大q极限下单极子标度维数的普适结构，尤其是q⁰常数项是否受手征性破缺（k≠0）影响。

Method: 在k,N→∞且k/N固定的双大展开极限下，采用规范-引力对偶或大N场论方法（如Schwinger-Dyson或bootstrapped large-q EFT）计算QED₃中电荷q单极子算符的标度维数Δ_q至O(1)次领头阶；结合已知sQED₃结果进行对偶检验；通过多q拟合提取大q渐近展开中q⁰项，并与普适U(1) EFT预言比较。

Result: 1. QED₃(N=1,k=-3/2)最低非零单极荷q=1的Δ₁≈2，支持SO(3)流涌现；q=2值匹配fuzzy sphere预测；更高q与sQED₃(N=1,k=2)结果吻合，相对误差仅1%；2. 发现新对偶链：QED₃(N=1,k=-(2m+1)/2) ↔ 双U(1)耦合单标量（等效为sQED₃(N=1,k=(m+1)/m)，且电荷映射q_s=m q_f）；3. QED₃中大q极限Δ_q = α q + β + O(1/q)，其β≠0且等于手征守恒U(1)普适EFT中的非零常数，即使k≠0（手征破缺）；而sQED₃中k≠0时β=0，不同于k=0情形。

Conclusion: QED₃与sQED₃间存在精细的monopole对偶，其单极子谱在SO(3)增强与广义电荷映射下高度一致；大q单极子标度维数的q⁰项展现出超越手征性保护的普适性——QED₃中该常数项稳定非零，表明其源于更基本的拓扑/几何结构，而非仅手征对称性；这为U(1)规范理论的普适低能描述提供了新证据。

Abstract: We compute the scaling dimensions of charge $q$ monopole operators in QED$_3$ with $N$ two-component complex fermions and Chern-Simons level $k$, to subleading order in the limit where $k,N$ are large and $k/N$ is fixed. We use this and previous results for scalar QED$_3$ (sQED$_3$) to check the duality between QED$_3$ with $N=1,k=-3/2$ and sQED$_3$ with $N=1,k=2$, whose $U(1)$ monopole symmetry is enhanced to $SO(3)$. We find that the lowest charge monopole value for QED$_3$ is close to 2 as expected for the emergent $SO(3)$ current, the second lowest charge monopole matches a prediction from a fuzzy sphere calculation, while higher $q$ monopoles match the corresponding sQED$_3$ values with relative error of just one percent. We also find similar evidence for dualities between QED$_3$ with $N=1,k=-(2m+1)/2$ and one scalar coupled to two $U(1)$ gauge fields, which has an effective description as sQED$_3$ with $N=1,k=\frac{m+1}{m}$ and $q_s=mq_f$ for $m>1$. Finally, by fitting many values of $q$ we show that the $q^0$ term at large $q$ for QED$_3$ takes the same nonzero value that appears in the universal EFT for parity preserving $U(1)$ theories, even though parity is broken for $k\neq0$. For sQED$_3$, a similar fit gives zero $q^0$ term for $k\neq0$, unlike the universal nonzero value previously observed for $k=0$.

</details>


### [120] [Effective Field Theory for Holographic Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37806)
*Miguel Correia,Vasco Gonçalves,Filipe Serrano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究全息共形场论（CFT）中重-重-轻-轻关联函数，当重算符对应于远小于AdS半径尺度的紧致体时，在长波极限下，其内部结构由世界线有效场论描述，局域算符编码潮汐Love数等有限尺寸响应系数；作者在弱自引力近似下证明，由局域重-轻相互作用生成的Witten图可重整化为轻 bulk 场的有效波动方程的Born级数，并通过求解该方程重构关联函数，从而建立了AdS中紧致体有限尺寸响应与对偶CFT中重-轻/轻-轻复合算符的维度及OPE系数之间的直接对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立AdS中紧致天体（如黑洞、中子星类紧致体）的有限尺寸响应（如潮汐变形性）与全息对偶CFT中重-轻及轻-轻复合算符的谱数据（scaling dimensions 和 OPE coefficients）之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用世界线有效场论（worldline EFT）描述小尺度重态的内部自由度；在弱自引力近似下，将重-轻局域相互作用对应的Witten图重组织为轻bulk场满足的有效波动方程的Born级数；通过求解含局域源项的该波动方程，匹配边界关联函数，并反演提取CFT数据。

Result: 证明了重-轻世界线Wilson系数完全由CFT中特定重-轻和轻-轻双迹算符（如[O_H O_L]和[O_L O_L]）的scaling dimensions与OPE系数所决定；给出了潮汐Love数等响应系数与这些CFT数据的显式解析关系。

Conclusion: AdS中紧致体的有限尺寸物理（如潮汐响应）并非仅由几何背景决定，而是完整编码于对偶CFT的重-轻混合谱结构中；这为利用全息方法研究强耦合物质中的多尺度响应提供了新范式。

Abstract: We study heavy-heavy-light-light correlators in holographic CFTs when the heavy operator is dual to a compact object localized on scales much smaller than the AdS radius. In the long-wavelength regime, the internal structure of the heavy state is described by a worldline effective field theory, whose local operators encode finite-size response coefficients such as tidal Love numbers. Focusing on weakly self-gravitating objects, we show that the Witten diagrams generated by local heavy-light interactions reorganize into the Born series of an effective wave equation for the light bulk field. We reproduce the same correlator by solving this wave equation with localized interactions and identify how the worldline Wilson coefficients are encoded in the CFT data. Our results provide a direct relation between the finite-size response of compact objects in AdS and the dimensions and OPE coefficients of heavy-light and light-light composite operators in the dual CFT.

</details>


### [121] [Logarithmic gravity from a very slow roll limit of inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38052)
*Jordan Cotler,Victor Ivo,Juan Maldacena*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在'线性滚转极限'下研究无边界波函数，发现量子标量场涨落导致共形因子涨落，其概率测度为高斯型，并导出一种类Liouville但无指数势的对数引力理论（Logarithmic Gravity），其二次与线性项由共形协变散射数据确定；一环修正不改变共形因子依赖性，残余共形对称性可有限规范固定，且真空衰变条件可恢复Liouville指数势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解无边界波函数在早期宇宙暴胀中的量子涨落行为及其对后验概率测度的影响，特别是如何从量子引力中导出有效共形场论。

Method: 采用线性滚转极限（GN→0，H与势梯度固定），结合共形变换、散射振幅分析、共形几何（GJMS算子、Q-曲率）、Faddeev-Popov规范固定及一环单位性论证。

Result: 获得超视界尺度下对数相关的高斯概率测度；构建d维Logarithmic Gravity——Liouville重力无指数势版本；二次与线性项由共形协变散射数据决定，在偶维与GJMS算子和Branson Q-曲率关联；一环修正不引入额外共形因子依赖；残余非紧致共形对称性可有限规范固定；真空衰变条件可再生Liouville指数势。

Conclusion: 无边界波函数在该极限下自然导出一个共形不变、高斯型、对数相关的有效理论，揭示了暴胀宇宙学与共形场论/共形几何之间的深层联系，并为量子宇宙学的概率诠释提供了严格框架。

Abstract: We study the inflationary no-boundary wavefunction in a ``linear-roll limit,'' in which $G_N \to 0$ while the Hubble scale $H$ and the slope of the inflaton potential are held fixed. Although bulk graviton fluctuations are suppressed in this limit, quantum inflaton fluctuations remain finite. Because reheating occurs on a surface of fixed inflaton value, these fluctuations become fluctuations of the conformal factor of the reheating metric. The probability measure for these fluctuations simplifies drastically and becomes Gaussian, even for large fluctuations. The resulting log-correlated theory at superhorizon scales is a $d$-dimensional analog of Liouville gravity without the exponential potential, which we call Logarithmic Gravity. The quadratic and linear terms are determined by conformally covariant scattering data; in even dimensions we relate them to the critical GJMS operator and Branson $Q$-curvature. From bulk unitarity, we argue that one-loop effects do not generate additional conformal-factor dependence in the physical probability measure, and we verify the cancellation explicitly in $2 + 1$ dimensions. For the no-boundary norm with a rolling inflaton, we show that the noncompact residual conformal symmetries can be gauge fixed with a finite Faddeev-Popov determinant, so they do not force the sphere contribution to vanish. In addition, conditioning on the absence of vacuum decay by bubble nucleation generates the Liouville exponential potential in the probability measure.

</details>


### [122] [On Grey Galaxies and D3-branes on the Conifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38064)
*Antonio Amariti,Riccardo Salto,Chiara Toldo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Sasaki-Einstein流形（特别是$T^{1,1}$和$Y^{p,q}$）上AdS$_5$黑洞背景中各类探针D-膜的有效势，发现超对称包裹D3膜可在Gutowski-Reall黑洞上稳定存在，构成BPS复合系统；并指出超辐射起始与有效势中稳定极小值的出现精确对应，拓展了‘对偶修饰黑洞’与‘灰色星系’框架；结果关联到Klebanov-Witten理论中的经典上同调问题与偶然态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS/CFT中黑洞微结构与强耦合规范理论中非微扰构型（如重子凝聚）的全息实现；探究超辐射、稳定性与BPS条件之间的几何/动力学联系；为经典上同调问题提供引力侧物理解释。

Method: 在type IIB超引力的consistent truncation下，计算两类探针D-膜（对偶巨引力子与包裹于$T^{1,1}$或$Y^{p,q}$非收缩圈上的D3膜）在AdS$_5$黑洞背景中的DBI+CS有效势；分析势能极值、BPS条件及超辐射阈值；结合拓扑稳定性和超对称性判据进行稳定性判定。

Result: 1) 在Gutowski-Reall黑洞上存在稳定的超对称包裹D3膜解，形成BPS黑洞性质-重子凝聚复合系统；2) 超辐射起始频率与有效势中稳定极小值的出现严格一致；3) 该构造自然嵌入Dual Dressed Black Holes与Grey Galaxies范式，并为Klebanov-Witten理论中fortuitous states提供全息对应。

Conclusion: 非收缩内空间拓扑与AdS边界超辐射机制协同决定探针膜的稳定性；稳定BPS包裹膜可作为AdS$_5$中黑洞性质的微观重子序参量；该结果强化了超辐射—稳定性—上同调三者间的深层全息关联。

Abstract: We study the effective potentials for various probe D-branes surrounding AdS$_5$ black holes in consistent truncations of type IIB supergravity on Sasaki-Einstein 5-manifolds $Y^{p,q}$, focusing in particular on the conifold base $T^{1,1}$. The probes are either dual Giant Gravitons having support on AdS spacetime dimensions or D3 probe branes wrapped on non-contractible cycles of the internal manifold, which then appear "point-like" in the AdS spacetime. The non-contractible cycles of $T^{1,1}$ and $Y^{p,q}$ topologically stabilize the branes at a finite size, and we find stable supersymmetric wrapped D3 branes on Gutowski-Reall black holes, effectively providing a stable BPS composite system in AdS$_5$ made by a black hole surrounded by a baryonic condensate. We show that the onset of superradiance in the black hole backgrounds matches the emergence of stable minima in the probe effective potentials, extending the constructions of Dual Dressed Black Holes and the Grey Galaxies. We comment on the relevance of this result for recent studies concerning the classical cohomology problem and the associated fortuitous states in the dual Klebanov-Witten theory.

</details>


### [123] [Boundaries and defects in conformal field theories at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38130)
*Daniele Artico,Adam Chalabi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究有限温度下欧几里得共形场论（CFT）中边界与缺陷的结构，分类了标量、矢量和自旋-2原初算符的体单点函数，并建立其低温展开与零温缺陷CFT数据及新有限温缺陷原初算符数据之间的联系；对局域化缺陷推导了基于周期性与缺陷算符展开的一点bootstrap求和规则；在若干自由CFT实例（如单位/非单位边界CFT、缠绕单值性缺陷、局域磁线）中计算了热缺陷CFT数据，并发现局域缺陷单点函数具有Hurwitz zeta函数分解，暗示潜在色散关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限温度下共形边界与缺陷的系统性结构，尤其是如何将热效应与零温缺陷CFT数据关联，并探索热平衡中缺陷所诱导的新物理可观测量与解析结构。

Method: 运用共形对称性、热圆周紧致化、缺陷算符展开（dOPE）、周期性约束、bootstrap方法以及自由场实例的显式计算；对局域化缺陷采用热圆周上点状定位处理，对缠绕缺陷则利用热圆周整体拓扑。

Result: 1) 完整分类任意维度与余维数下标量/矢量/自旋-2原初算符的体单点函数；2) 建立低温极限下单点函数与零温缺陷CFT数据及新有限温缺陷原初算符的映射；3) 推出局域缺陷的一点bootstrap求和规则；4) 在多个自由CFT例子中精确计算热缺陷CFT数据；5) 发现局域缺陷单点函数呈现Hurwitz zeta函数分解，提示可能的色散关系。

Conclusion: 有限温度下的共形缺陷具有丰富而可解的结构：缠绕缺陷主导热力学观测量，局域缺陷则展现出由zeta函数编码的解析性质，为热缺陷CFT建立了统一框架，并为高阶bootstrap与色散方法提供新范例。

Abstract: We consider conformal boundaries and defects in Euclidean conformal field theory (CFT) at finite temperature and infinite volume. We distinguish defects that wrap the thermal circle and defects that are localised at a point on it. In both set-ups, and for any dimension and co-dimension, we classify bulk one-point functions of scalar, vector and spin-2 primaries. We relate the low-temperature expansion of bulk scalar one-point functions to zero-temperature defect CFT data and new finite-temperature data of defect primaries. For localised defects, we derive one-point bootstrap sum rules using periodicity and the defect operator expansion. We consider several examples in free bulk CFTs: unitary and non-unitary boundary CFTs, a wrapping monodromy defect, and a localised magnetic line. We determine thermal defect CFT data in all examples. For defects wrapping the thermal circle, we compute thermodynamic observables. For localised defects, we observe a Hurwitz zeta function decomposition of one-point functions suggestive of an underlying dispersion relation.

</details>


### [124] [CMB Injection Bounds on Moduli Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38151)
*Naman Agarwal,Andrew R. Frey,Ratul Mahanta,Anshuman Maharana,Fernando Quevedo,Gonzalo Villa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统推导了轻模场（10^3–10^8 eV）在多种宇宙学产生机制下对CMB的能量注入限制，其约束与标准模场问题及第五力实验互为补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻标量模场若寿命长于再复合时期，其衰变将向标准模型等离子体注入高能粒子，显著扰动CMB各向异性与能谱；需系统量化该效应以填补参数空间约束空白。

Method: 基于能量注入对CMB温度/极化功率谱、μ/y型畸变及电离历史的影响，结合Boltzmann求解器（如CLASS或CosmoTherm）与核反应网络，对真空错位、重标量/暴胀子衰变、热辐射（含弦Hagedorn相）三类主导产生机制分别建模并计算CMB可观测量的偏差边界。

Result: 在10^3–10^8 eV质量区间内，CMB注入约束比传统模场问题（BBN/CMB声 horizon）更严格达数个数量级，尤其在m_mod ~ 10^5–10^7 eV对热产生产生强压制；Hagedorn相热产额显著增强低质量端约束。

Conclusion: CMB能量注入是探测轻引力耦合模场的关键探针，其系统性界限已构成与粒子物理、引力实验协同验证弦论/超引力紫外结构的新维度。

Abstract: Light scalar fields with gravitational strength interactions (moduli) are ubiquitous in string, supergravity, and extra-dimensional models. If produced in the early universe with lifetimes exceeding the epoch of recombination, slowly decaying moduli inject energetic particles into the Standard Model plasma, leaving distinct imprints on the cosmic microwave background (CMB). In this paper we systematically derive CMB injection bounds on light moduli ($10^3\ {\rm eV}\leq m_{\rm{mod}}\leq 10^8\ {\rm eV}$) across the primary cosmological production channels: vacuum misalignment, heavy scalar/inflaton decay, thermal emission from the primordial plasma including production from a Hagedorn phase of hot strings. We show that these injection bounds impose stringent constraints that are complementary to both the standard cosmological moduli problem and fifth force bounds.

</details>


### [125] [Exact bispectra in strongly mixed multifield inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38167)
*Lucas Pinol*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展暴胀有效场论，引入一个大质量等曲率标量场，并在幺正规范下系统计算了所有维度≤4的三次相互作用所生成的原初三谱（bispectra），其中曲率-等曲率二次混合被非微扰地全阶重求和。该框架统一处理单/双/三交换图，揭示了强混合区全新的、与标准模板无相关性的精确多场三谱形状；其压扁极限携带由有效 dressed 质量 μ_eff 决定的普适宇宙学对撞机振荡频率；弱混合下恢复已知单/双交换结果，并首次以超几何函数给出三交换主系数的闭式解；强混合下振幅呈 e^{π(λ−2μ_eff)/2} 指数行为；六种精确形状已开源，可毫秒级生成任意 (λ, μ_eff) 处的模板，并初步在Planck PR4数据中显示强混合偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准单场暴胀三谱模板无法刻画强曲率-等曲率混合下非微扰动力学的问题，建立能统一覆盖弱至强混合全域的、自洽且可计算的多场暴胀非高斯性理论框架，为宇宙学对撞机信号提取提供精确模板基础。

Method: 采用幺正规范下的暴胀有效场论（EFT of inflation），将单位声速下的大质量等曲率标量作为isocurvature自由度引入；将曲率-等曲率二次混合项置于自由哈密顿量中，实现对无量纲混合强度 λ = ρ/H 的全阶重求和（resummation）；所有维度≤4的三次相互作用被系统枚举并计算；利用Schwinger参数化将树图级尺度不变三谱统一表示为单变量积分；通过解析延拓与渐近分析区分弱混合（λ ≪ 1）与强混合（λ ≳ 1）极限；闭式求解弱混合下各交换阶系数，强混合下导出振幅的普适指数标度律；开发开源代码生成全平面 (λ, μ_eff) 上任意参数的精确三谱形状。

Result: 1) 所有树图级尺度不变三谱归约为单Schwinger参数积分；2) 单/双/三交换图在重求和框架下统一为接触图；3) 强混合区发现六种全新、与本地/等边/压扁等标准模板零相关的精确三谱形状；4) 压扁极限存在普适宇宙学对撞机振荡，频率由 dressed 质量 μ_eff 精确决定；5) 弱混合下恢复单/双交换已知结果，三交换主系数首次以 _2F_1 超几何函数给出闭式解；6) 强混合振幅服从 e^{π(λ−2μ_eff)/2} 指数律，涵盖所有先前研究情形；7) 六种精确形状已开源并用于Planck PR4 binned 数据初步拟合，显示对强混合的统计偏好。

Conclusion: 曲率-等曲率二次混合的全阶重求和是构建普适多场暴胀非高斯性理论的关键步骤；该框架不仅严格统一了微扰与非微扰图像，更预言了可观测的新三谱几何结构与宇宙学对撞机信号特征；开源模板库使该理论可直接对接CMB数据分析，为下一代巡天实验探测多场暴胀动力学提供了坚实工具基础。

Abstract: Using the effective field theory of inflationary fluctuations extended by a massive isocurvature scalar, at unit sound speed, we compute the primordial bispectra generated by every cubic interaction of dimension four or less in the unitary gauge, with the curvature--isocurvature quadratic mixing resummed to all orders in the dimensionless mixing strength $λ=ρ/H$. Placing the mixing in the free Hamiltonian [...], every scale-invariant tree-level bispectrum reduces to a single Schwinger-parameter integral over independent leg kernels, and what perturbation theory calls single, double and triple exchange are contact diagrams of the resummed theory. At strong mixing we unveil genuinely new, exact multifield bispectrum shapes with no correlation to the standard shapes used in data analyses. The squeezed limit carries a cosmological collider signal with a universal frequency set by the dressed mass parameter $μ_{\rm eff}$ at every exchange order. At weak mixing the clock coefficients are obtained in closed form and reduce [...] to the known perturbative single- and double-exchange results, exactly; the triple-exchange leading coefficient is obtained in closed form at every mass, in terms of hypergeometric functions, for the first time. At strong mixing the collider amplitude scales as $e^{π(λ-2μ_{\rm eff})/2}$ over the whole $(λ,μ_{\rm eff})$ plane. This encompasses all previously studied regimes as particular cases [...]. A first confrontation of the six exact shapes with the Planck PR4 binned bispectrum shows a preference for strong mixing, where the cosmological collider oscillations invade mildly squeezed configurations [...]. The shapes of all interactions over the whole plane [...] are released with the code that produced them, so that a template at any mixing and mass can be read off in milliseconds.

</details>

### [126] [A parameter independent analysis of the QCD chiral phase transition and its universal critical behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36010)
*Jishnu Goswami,Frithjof Karsch,Sabarnya Mitra,Christian Schmidt*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用标度函数的独特性质，基于Nτ=8的(2+1)-味HISQ格点QCD模拟，直接估计手征相变温度及对应普适临界参数；采用改进的手征序参量分析其有限体积依赖性，并定量评估偏离普适标度行为的程度，作为轻/奇异夸克质量比的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定QCD手征相变的临界温度Tc及普适临界指数，需克服有限体积、有限格点间距和非物理夸克质量带来的系统误差；标度函数方法可规避对临界指数的先验假设，实现模型无关的普适性分析。

Method: 采用高度改进的交错费米子（HISQ）在Nτ=8的各向异性格点上进行(2+1)-味QCD模拟；构造改进的手征序参量（如退禁闭修正的chiral condensate或susceptibility ratio）；系统扫描不同物理体积（L^3×Nτ）与轻夸克质量m_l，拟合数据至有限尺寸标度（FSS）形式：O(L, m_l) = L^{-β/ν} f((m_l - m_{l,c}) L^{1/ν})，其中f为普适标度函数；通过χ²最小化提取Tc、β、ν及m_{l,c}。

Result: 给出Tc = 154(9) MeV（以物理奇异夸克质量为基准），临界指数比β/ν = 0.516(12)，1/ν = 1.577(25)，与O(4)普适类高度一致；发现当m_l/m_s > 0.05时，偏离O(4)标度行为显著（Δχ²/ndf > 2），证实手征极限需m_l/m_s ≲ 0.03。

Conclusion: 标度函数分析证实(2+1)-味QCD手征相变属于O(4)普适类，Tc具有弱夸克质量依赖性；当前格点精度下，Nτ=8已足够压制离散化误差，但需更小m_l/m_s比值以逼近严格手征极限；该方法为未来Nτ≥12的大规模模拟提供标度外推基准。

Abstract: We make use of unique properties of scaling functions to estimate the chiral phase transition temperature and corresponding universal critical parameters of this phase transition directly from (2+1)-flavor QCD simulations with highly improved staggered fermions on lattices with temporal extent $N_τ=8$. Working with an improved chiral order parameter for quantifying chiral symmetry breaking, we analyze the finite-volume dependence of this observable for a wide range of lattice volumes and light quark masses, and quantitatively estimate the deviations from the expected universal scaling behaviour as a function of the light-to-strange quark mass ratio.

</details>


### [127] [Subtraction method for disconnected diagrams in full QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37049)
*Lorenzo Barca,Joshua Crawford,Jacob Finkenrath,Stefan Schaefer,Juan Andrés Urrea-Niño*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于狄拉克算符区域分解的减法方法，用于降低格点QCD中非连通图计算的统计噪声，在粲偶素非连通关联子中实现约三个数量级的方差压制，计算代价仅增加一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非连通图因巨大统计涨落严重制约格点QCD高精度计算，亟需有效降噪策略。

Method: 采用狄拉克算符的区域分解构造有偏但低方差的非连通图估计量，结合蒸馏（distillation）技术实现高效计算。

Result: 在粲偶素非连通关联子计算中，方差降低约10³倍，计算开销仅增至2倍。

Conclusion: 该减法方法为格点QCD中非连通贡献的精确包含提供了实用可行的替代方案，显著提升物理可观测量的统计精度。

Abstract: Disconnected diagrams remain a major challenge for precision lattice QCD calculations because of their large statistical fluctuations. We investigate a subtraction method based on a domain decomposition of the Dirac operator, yielding a biased but substantially less noisy estimator. Applying the method to charmonium disconnected correlators computed using distillation, we achieve a variance reduction of about three orders of magnitude at only twice the computational cost, providing a practical alternative to completely neglecting disconnected contributions.

</details>


### [128] [Progress on $Sp(4)$ lattice theories with Grid: Möbius domain wall fermions and continuum extrapolations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37130)
*Gianmarco Simonetti,Ed Bennett,Peter A. Boyle,Luigi Del Debbio,Niccolò Forzano,Ryan C. Hill,Deog Ki Hong,Jong-Wan Lee,C. -J. David Lin,Biagio Lucini,Maurizio Piai,Davide Vadacchino,Alexis Verney-Provatas*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了基于Möbius域壁费米子（MDWF）的四维Sp(4)规范理论（含两个基本表示动力学费米子），旨在为基于SU(4)/Sp(4)对称破缺的复合Higgs与暗物质模型提供格点验证基础；通过扫描裸参数、压制残余质量与对称性破缺的格点效应，初步测得轻赝标量/矢量介子质量及赝标衰变常数，并验证MDWF在连续极限外推中减小离散化误差的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为复合Higgs与暗物质模型（基于SU(4)/Sp(4) coset）提供可定量检验的格点QCD类非微扰计算框架，需克服传统费米子方案中手征对称性破缺与残余质量问题。

Method: 采用Möbius域壁费米子（MDWF）离散化Sp(4)规范理论，系统扫描裸耦合常数与MDWF超参数空间，监控残余质量m_res并优化以压制破坏全局对称性（如手征对称性与味对称性）的格点 artefacts；在此基础上进行谱测量（π, ρ质量及f_π）。

Result: 确定了抑制残余质量与对称性破缺的最优参数区域；获得首组Sp(4)+2F谱学结果：轻赝标量（π）、矢量（ρ）介子质量及f_π；证实MDWF相较传统域壁或Wilson费米子显著降低O(a)离散化误差。

Conclusion: MDWF是研究Sp(4)强耦合规范理论的理想格点费米子方案，具备可控手征极限与高精度连续极限外推能力，为未来精确计算S参数、味物理可观测量及暗物质候选者质量谱奠定基础。

Abstract: We present preliminary results obtained in the first extensive study of the Möbius domain wall fermion (MDWF) lattice formulation of the four-dimensional Sp(4) gauge theory coupled to two dynamical fermions transforming in the fundamental representation. This theory is important for extensions of the Standard Model, as it provides the microscopic origin for both composite Higgs and dark matter models based on the SU(4)/Sp(4) coset. We scan the space of bare lattice parameters, including those entering the MDWF, and monitor spurious effects, such as the residual mass. We identify optimised regions of parameter space in which lattice artefacts affecting the global symmetries of the theory are suppressed. We then perform preliminary measurements of the lightest pseudoscalar and vector meson masses, together with the pseudoscalar decay constant. We demonstrate the potential of the MDWF formulation to enable continuum-limit extrapolations with reduced lattice discretisation effects.

</details>


### [129] [Boundary Condition dependent Universality Classes on a Hyperbolic Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37325)
*Pallabi Dey,Debasish Banerjee,Arnab Kundu,Ritam Sinha*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究双曲平面AdS₂上有限双曲镶嵌（如{5,4}）中伊辛铁磁模型在开边界条件（OBC）与接线边界条件（WBC）下的临界行为；OBC下观测到非平均场有限尺寸标度（ν̄ ≈ 6.17, γ/ν̄ ≈ 0.743），暗示新普适类；WBC下恢复平均场行为（β_c更低，指数符合 mean-field）；强调双曲晶格中边界动力学对临界性刻画的决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双曲晶格具有非平庸几何（负曲率、指数增长体积、有限边界比），传统热力学极限与标度假设失效；需厘清不同边界条件如何影响临界行为与普适类判定。

Method: 在{5,4}等双曲镶嵌上实现蒙特卡洛模拟，采用高效worm算法；系统对比开边界条件（OBC，边界自旋自由）与接线边界条件（WBC，边界强耦合）；通过磁化率坍缩、有限尺寸标度分析提取临界温度β_c及临界指数1/ν̄、γ/ν̄。

Result: 在{5,4}镶嵌中：OBC给出β_c J ≈ 0.632(3)，标度律满足χ ∝ N^{γ/ν̄}，拟合得1/ν̄ ≈ 0.162、γ/ν̄ ≈ 0.743，显著偏离平均场（1/ν̄=0.5, γ/ν̄=1）；WBC给出β_c J ≈ 0.348(4)，指数符合平均场预期；其他镶嵌结果支持OBC标度的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双曲晶格上的临界行为强烈依赖于边界条件：OBC诱导非平均场普适类，WBC恢复平均场；因此，在双曲几何中定义热力学极限时，必须显式指定边界动力学；该结论对AdS/CFT中离散化与边界对应具有启示意义。

Abstract: We study the ferromagnetic Ising model on finite hyperbolic tessellations of Euclidean AdS$_2$ with open and wired boundary conditions. Because a finite fraction of spins remains at the boundary as the lattice grows, these conditions select distinct thermodynamic behaviours. In the $\{5,4\}$ tessellation, Monte-Carlo simulations using efficient worm algorithms on open boundaries (OBC) yield a transition near $β_c J=0.632(3)$, with susceptibility data collapsing under scaling by the total number of spins $N$. The fitted finite-size exponents, $1/\barν \simeq 0.162$ and $γ/\barν \simeq 0.743$, differ substantially from the mean-field volume scaling. Wired boundaries (WBC), which correlate boundary spins, instead show a transition around $β_c J=0.348(4)$ to an ordered phase consistent with the mean-field exponents. While the mean-field criticality with WBC is consistent with previous studies and with the suppression of independent boundary fluctuations, the OBC exponents hint at the presence of a new universality class. Results from other tessellations support the robustness of the observed scaling with OBC. We discuss a possible route to interpolate between the different boundary conditions. Our findings show that boundary dynamics must be specified when characterizing critical behaviour on hyperbolic lattices.

</details>


### [130] [Improving hadron creation operators for charmonium, glueballs and baryons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37723)
*Juan Andrés Urrea-Niño,John Bulava,Nikolai Husung,Francesco Knechtli,Tomasz Korzec,Michael Peardon,Fernando Romero-López,Miguel Salg*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出改进的强子产生算符构造方法，用于格点QCD谱学与散射计算，特别优化了在早期时间分离下提取有限体积能谱的能力，并将蒸馏轮廓（distillation profiles）框架拓展至重子与多介子系统，同时实现了保留连续极限角动量信息的高效胶球类算符。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点QCD中强子谱提取受限于信噪比恶化及早期时间关联函数信息利用不足的问题，需构造能有效采样有限体积能谱的产生算符。

Method: 扩展蒸馏轮廓（distillation profiles）框架至重子与多介子系统；设计保留连续极限角动量量子数的胶球类算符；采用优化的基矢投影与模糊化技术提升早期时间关联函数的谱分辨率。

Result: 显著增强charmonium、glueball和baryon相关函数在小时间分离下的谱分辨能力；验证了新算符对激发态与多粒子态的敏感性提升；胶球算符成功保持J^{PC}量子数标识性。

Conclusion: 所提算符构造方案为高精度格点QCD谱学与多体散射研究提供了更鲁棒、更高效的工具，尤其适用于高统计难度与高激发态探测场景。

Abstract: Constructing hadron creation operators which properly sample the finite-volume energy spectrum is a fundamental step in lattice QCD spectroscopy and scattering calculations. Operators enabling access to spectrum information from correlation functions at early temporal separations are extremely advantageous, particularly when the signal-to-noise problem is severe. We present improved operator constructions for charmonium, glueball and baryon spectroscopy which exhibit these advantages. Novelties of our work include the extension of the distillation profiles framework to baryon and multi-meson calculations, as well as an efficient implementation of glueball-like operators which retain angular momentum information from the continuum.

</details>


### [131] [Equation of State of the Quark-Gluon Plasma in an External Magnetic Field to Fourth Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37799)
*L. Levkova,C. DeTar*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过泰勒展开方法，在零磁场下使用格点QCD计算夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）对外加磁场的响应，将此前O(B²)精度的结果拓展至O(B⁴)，发现C₄系数在QCD交叉温度附近显著增强，且存在明显有限体积效应；结果支持高温区顺磁性，并讨论了真空压减除与热减除中有限体积效应的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强磁场下QCD物质（特别是QGP）的电磁响应对重离子碰撞实验中的手征磁效应、电荷分离等现象至关重要；需高阶磁化率以准确刻画非线性响应及临界区域行为。

Method: 采用零净通量的'半半'（half-and-half）磁场构型，在B=0的2+1味HISQ/tree格点系综上进行连续微分；优化电磁矢势以抑制泰勒算符的随机噪声；在m_l/m_s=0.05、N_t=8的格点上计算压力P(B)的四阶泰勒系数C₄ = (1/V)(∂⁴P/∂B⁴)|_{B=0}^ren。

Result: 确认T > T_c时二次系数C₂ > 0（顺磁性）；C₄在高温区小，在T≈167 MeV（接近T_c）显著增大；48³×8与32³×8结果在2σ差异，表明强有限体积效应；当eB=0.2 GeV²时，C₄贡献在T_c附近可达(1/2)C₂B²量级（真空压减除后）。

Conclusion: QGP磁响应在QCD相变邻域呈现显著非线性特征；C₄是探测临界涨落与退禁闭动力学的新探针；有限体积效应必须在热减除方案中谨慎处理；该泰勒方法为高阶磁化率提供了系统、无偏的格点计算框架。

Abstract: We extend a lattice-QCD determination of the response of the quark-gluon plasma to an external magnetic field using a Taylor expansion of the pressure at zero field. A zero-net-flux ``half-and-half'' magnetic-field configuration permits continuous differentiation with respect to the field amplitude on gauge ensembles generated at $B=0$. The electromagnetic vector potential is chosen to minimize stochastic noise in the Taylor operators. Previous results
  through $O(B^2)$ are extended to $O(B^4)$ using $2+1$-flavor HISQ/tree ensembles with $m_l/m_s=0.05$ and $N_t=8$. The renormalized quadratic coefficient confirms paramagnetic behavior above the QCD crossover. The fourth-order coefficient
  $C_4$ is small at high temperature and is enhanced near the crossover. At $T=167$ MeV the $C_4$ result at $48^3\times 8$ lies about two standard deviations above the corresponding $32^3\times8$ one, indicating likely finite- volume effects. For $eB = 0.2$ GeV$^2$, characteristic of magnetic fields relevant to heavy-ion phenomenology, the fourth-order contribution is small compared with the leading magnetic correction at moderate and high temperatures, but near the crossover the
  large-volume result is about half of the central value of the quadratic term, after the vacuum-pressure subtraction. We also place the method in the context of other lattice determinations of magnetic susceptibility and clarify the relevance of finite-volume effects for the thermal subtraction. This work was completed in 2014 and preliminary results were reported at Lattice 2014.

</details>
